GLEIF's legal name and ultimate parent when known; where the grouping comes from: `sources`; then, where the data has them (older files: none), "Ownership": the curated notes of `companies.json` `ownership` (a group's, or on a company page its groups' notes on its own names, and every note of a group it alone makes up, as an old name need not be a holder name of its medicines (AcelRx on Talphera's page); the holders of one note together: "Wyeth Europa Ltd, Wyeth Lederle Vaccines S.A.: Wyeth acquired by Pfizer in 2009.", `companies.ownership()`), then its medicines' notes (the name a link to its card): those a per-medicine row put under their current owner, the owner named unless it is the page's group ("[Panretin] (under Advanz Pharma): {group_note}." on Eisai's page, "[Trudexa]: …" on AbbVie's), and plain notes on a medicine's later ownership ("[Udenyca]: {group_note}."), each followed by a "Source" link to its evidence ("en.wikipedia.org" named "Wikipedia", "www.sec.gov" "SEC website")). A company key (`c.*`) shows that company alone. On phones the company links of holder displays (padding with a negative margin, as the medicine names) and the company page's name and ATC links are 44px targets. **Search**: a "Companies" suggestion group (last, or first when the query names a group: its name, monogram or another name exactly, `named`; `suggestCompanies()`: group name, monogram, then the names that lead to a group: its companies, their spellings, the EMA holder names of its medicines and the old names of its `ownership` notes ("acelrx": Talphera, "matches “AcelRx Pharmaceuticals”"), named as "matches “…”"; meta "{n} authorized"); picking one, or Enter on a query that names it ("msd", "bms", "pfizer" + the phone's Search key; `submitChoice()` takes an option's `named`), opens the company page ("janssen" is only a word of J&J's other names: Enter still searches the indication texts). **Aggregated views by group**: the company breakdown, "Who is active where" rows and "Stack by: Company" (see each). About says the company groups show the current owner as curated here (as of their date), not an official record, and credits GLEIF (CC0, "GLEIF does not provide or endorse this site"). Copy: `UI.companies`; "Holder" became "Company" on the breakdown and stack toggles and the filter chip ("Company | 2 selected", "Remove company filter: …"; a row path names its group, "Merck Sharp & Dohme B.V. (Organon)", `companies.label()`; an EMA holder name says so, as a company can have the same name: "Roche Registration GmbH (EMA holder name)"; one company's rows under several groups name the company, `selectionName()`). Aggregated names (tooltips, bar and row names, the legend's explanations) leave out "Not stated" (`holderNames()`), and are left out where they only repeat the row's own name ("Teva B.V., 38 medicines", not "…: Teva B.V."; `namesBehind()`); a row named after one of several names lists the others after "also" ("Roche Registration GmbH, 41 medicines: also Roche Registration Ltd., Roche Registration Limited"; `UI.companies.legalNames(names, own)`).
- **Modality** (M2 phase 2, spec 2026-09-29, `.remember/modality/modality-spec.md` §11; owner-side decisions 2026-09-29: Peptide inside "Protein and peptide", static rows count-only, no new modalities): `modalities.js` (pure, tested; `buildModalityTree()` of `modalities.json`: groups › modalities in tree order, Small molecule a group without modalities whose rows carry modality `small_molecule`), `modality-tree.js` (the `createFacetTree()` spec, `#facet-modality` after Medicine type; a chip's popover or sheet borrows it). Names and explainers (≤ 12 words, U.S. spelling, "kind" as "type" is the medicine type) are `UI.modalities` and `UI.modalityTips` in `labels.js`, keyed by the taxonomy's keys (a test checks both key sets against the spec's taxonomy and, when present, `modalities.json`). A product (`buildProducts()`) has `modalityRows` (per substance), `modalityKeys` (every group and modality of its substances; the filter, tree, breakdown and stack) and `modalityExact` (its static rows: a group none of whose sources names the modality, `NOT_CLASSIFIED` for a substance no source classifies or no row); a medicine counts once in every group and modality (children need not add up). Tree: groups (count) › modalities (count), under a group with modalities a static "not more specific" row, last a static "Not classified" row (facet-tree `rootStatic`), both count-only with an explainer (`staticTip`: a tooltip spanning the row, `tabindex="-1"` for a tap, the text visually hidden after the count); rows explained as the area tree's (data-tip on the row, `modalityTipId()` hidden descriptions made by `renderTypeTips()`, at the pointer on hover and, with keyboard focus, across the section in a popover or sheet: the `#facet-area` tooltip rules also cover `#facet-modality`), but unlike the MeSH notes a tap shows them on touch screens (`hover: none`), as the type and status tips (review 2026-09-29: they are at most 12 words): the facet-tree spec's `tapTip` and `renderBreakdown()`'s `tapTip` option add `.tap-tip`, which the touch rule hiding MeSH tips skips, and a tap on such a carrier does not hide the tips (`setupTips()`; a mouse click still does, desktop unchanged); after a tap drills the breakdown (or "Up one level" moves focus to the bars), the first new bar has focus without its tip (bug hunt 2026-10-01: L01's 104px tip covered L04 and L03, so L read as having two subclasses; `focusQuietly()` in `main.js`: `.tip-quiet`, shown again by keyboard focus), after Enter with it; a tap on a bar drills (on a leaf's, or one shown alone, focus goes to "Up one level"), so on a touch screen no bar shows its explanation: the classes' and modalities' explanations show there in the filter trees only (open question for the owner, bug hunt 2026-10-01 fix-up review: the first new bar's tip again, which hides the next bars, or the drilled class's explanation elsewhere, e.g. on the path's last item); checkbox states checked / included (under a checked group) / mixed; "Find a modality" matches names and keys from 3 characters ("adc", "car t"). Medicine card: a "Modality" fact after Type, one line per distinct modality of its substances (several: each names its substances, "insulin degludec: …"), "{Group} › {Modality}" as links to `modalityState()`, the explainer as muted text, then "Source: …" (`modalitySource()`: "WHO INN stem “-siran”", "WHO INN group “insulin”", "Radionuclide in its INN (177Lu)", "WHO INN naming of proteins (Greek letter “alfa”)" (rule `inn:greek second word (alfa)`: ATryn, thrombin alfa), "ChEMBL CHEMBL941 (ChEMBL_37)" linking to its ChEMBL page (www.ebi.ac.uk, "ChEMBL website"), "WHO ATC class {atcClassLabel()}", "EMA: advanced therapy medicinal product", "EMA text naming a live vaccine" (the rule's words, `TEXT_RULE_WORDS` in `labels.js`; the data's rule values unchanged), "{document} (checked by hand)", the document a link to the curated evidence named by `evidenceDocument()`: product information, EPAR, refusal report, withdrawal assessment report (also one filed as a variation report, Skycovion's), scientific discussion, EMA questions and answers, CHMP summary of opinion, EMA medicine page, PubMed, ChEMBL; another page by its host; a data-file test checks every source and evidence of `ema_medicine_modalities.json` is named), then "; kind from …" when `leaf_source` named the modality; "{Group}, not more specific" or "Not classified" with their explainers. Substance card: one "Modality" fact (the modality its medicines' rows for that substance give most often) in its Status block. On the medicine card the Modality fact is in More details (F · Spacious, phase 4). Result tables: no Modality column (it did not fit the five columns and phone rows; card only). Breakdown `by=mod`: groups (most first, ties in tree order; the "Tree" sort is tree order), then a static "Not classified" row; one value selected (`drillModality()`): a group's modalities and its "not more specific" row, a modality or Small molecule alone; "Up one level" and the path (`renderModalityPath()`); bars stacked by medicine type as the ATC bars, explained on hover and keyboard focus (on a touch screen a tap drills: see above). "Approvals per year" Stack by Modality: the groups in tree order in `STACK_HUES` mids (`facets.js`; neighbours ≥ the series distance and every mid ≥ 3:1 in both modes, `palette.test.js`), then "Not classified" low-key (`STACK_OTHER`); with exactly one group selected its modalities, then "{Group}, not more specific" low-key; only the series present, each legend entry explained; a medicine counts in each group (or modality) it has (the note says so); `withUnplaced()` keeps every mode's yearly totals equal. About's sources: "Modalities from WHO INN stems (World Health Organization, 2024), read as facts in our own words, ChEMBL molecule types and EMA data, some checked by hand.", once `meta.json` credits "WHO INN stems" (no NC licence named: the stems are facts; legal review 2026-09-30). "Who is active where" columns by modality: not built (optional follow-up).
- **Facet-count rule** (`facets.js`, tested): a facet counts the medicines matching every *other* active filter (`facetCounts()`: `filterProducts(…, dimension)`; phase 4a dropped the per-view populations); Status is a filter like any other, authorized by default (owner decision 2026-09-29), so by default every facet but Status counts the authorized medicines, and Status, its own filter left out, every status. `filterProducts()`'s `except` takes a dimension or a list of them. A product counts once per value; selected values stay listed at count 0. The ATC tree, breakdown and class-path counts are the medicines matching every filter but the ATC one (the breakdown's area and holder modes: every filter but their own); so are the therapeutic area tree's, but for the area filter, and the company tree's, but for the company filter. Ladder and class-suggestion counts = currently authorized, no filters.
- **The ATC code the site uses** (phase 4c review; the data contract since phase 4b/4d): every view groups, filters, counts and shows an ATC row by `atcCode()` in `atc.js` = `current_atc_code` ?? `atc_code` ?? `atc_code_human` (older data files), so retired codes count under the class WHO moved them to (L01XC02 under L01FA01) and products without an EMA code (`atc_code_human` null, 16 on 2026-09-27) under their product-information code; rows with no code at all are skipped (they used to throw and blank every card after the breakdown). "incomplete" (the flag in the tables, the chip on the card) = `atcRowIncomplete()`: `!atc_final_level` (phase 4e: Rienso's curated B03AC, which WHO does not subdivide, and J07BX03 moved up to J07BN are complete), for older data files not a valid level-5 code (`atcIncomplete()`). Where it differs from EMA's published code, `atcOrigin()` says how ("retired" / "completed" / "conflict" / "smpc" / "curated") and `atcOriginText()` / `atcOriginFlag()` in `labels.js` say it: in the medicines table and result tables a short flag under the badge, one per line (user decision 2026-09-28; "was L01XC02", "EMA: L01XL", "SmPC"; aria-hidden) with the sentence in the badge's tooltip (`atcBadgeTip()`, a data-tip since 2026-09-29, see "Type explanations") and visually hidden ("L01XC02 Rituximab: retired 2022, now L01FA01."; the year from `atc_classes.json` `changed_year`); on the medicine card the sentence under that code's ladder. Curated codes (phase 4e, `atc_code_source` "curated") name their evidence, found from `atc_code_document_url` (an ATC/DDD Index page, WHO's temporary list, an EMA PDF): "EMA publishes N07; this code was added from the WHO ATC index." / "…the full code was added from WHO's temporary list (it can still change)." / "…from the SmPC text." (`UI.atc.evidence`; flag "EMA: {code}", or "WHO index" / "WHO temporary" / "SmPC text" without an EMA code); the medicine card adds the evidence as an external link ("WHO ATC index page", "WHO temporary list (Excel)", "Product information (SmPC)"; atcddd.fhi.no is the "WHOCC website"). The source line of the badge's tooltip (both tables since 2026-09-29) names `atc_code_source` ("EMA product information (SmPC)", "checked by hand (WHO ATC index, WHO temporary list or SmPC text)"). The ATC tree says so in its note.
- **Failing parts stay contained** (phase 4c review): `safely()` in `main.js` renders each dashboard part (the type and status lists, year filter, filter chips, tree, headline, tiles, each card, table) on its own: an exception is logged and that part shows "Not available right now." (`.card-error`) until a render succeeds; the lookup panel does the same for its card instead of staying on "Loading…".
- **One dashboard, authorized by default** (F · Spacious, phase 3: "Authorized over time" leads with a takeaway, its subtitle and note in its (i) panel; see "Card polish") (phase 4a, user decision: no "Authorized now" / "Approvals per year" tabs and no `view`; owner decision 2026-09-29, "Authorized by default": "1,567 of 2,351 medicines in the EMA data are currently authorized in the EU." confused non-experts, "why are there medicines that are not authorized?"): the population is every medicine matching the filters, and the Status filter is Authorised by default (see URL keys), so the overview (headline, charts, tables, counts, facets, CSV) shows the authorized medicines unless the viewer widens it; search and lookup views (medicine, substance, condition, company pages, indication-text search) still find and show every medicine and say why one is not authorized (unchanged). The default is no active filter: the Status chip stays inactive and "N active filters" and the headline's forms ignore it; Clear filters, a popover's or sheet's Clear and the Status chip's remove button return to it (`activeFilterCount()` in `url.js`, the one count; F · Spacious, phase 1 replaced the sidebar's "Reset all" and the sentence's Reset). Headline (`UI.headline`) by default (`UI.headline.authorized`), counting the medicines with status Authorised as the dashboard lists them, the 6 without an approval date included: no other filter "{N} medicines are authorized EU-wide through EMA." (1,573 on 2026-09-28); other filters "{N} authorized medicines match these filters." (385; one and none handled); one ATC class "{N} authorized medicines in L04AC Interleukin Inhibitors." (33), one therapeutic area "{N} authorized medicines in Neoplasms." (386; a root tag "tagged Cancer"). By default, inline after the headline in the page header's lead paragraph (F · Spacious mockup; `.page-lead`: `#headline` is a span in it), a quiet sentence in place of the breakdown by every status (`#status-scope`, muted, `UI.statusScope.more()`): "EMA's list also has 778 medicines that are not authorized: withdrawn, refused, expired or awaiting a decision." (other filters: "163 more medicines matching these filters are not authorized: …"; the medicines of other statuses matching the other filters, `filterProducts(…, "status")` less those shown; 778 = 2,351 − 1,573, not the owner's 784 = 2,351 − 1,567, as the 6 undated authorized ones are listed; hidden at 0), then "Include them" (`#status-include`, `.lead-button`: reads as a link, 44px tall on phones; named "Include them: show medicines of every status"): every status, focus to the headline. With every status (`?status=all`): no other filter "{N} medicines in the EMA data, {A} of them currently authorized." (2,351 and 1,567); other filters "{N} medicines match these filters, {A} of them currently authorized." (none / all / one handled); one ATC class or one therapeutic area as before ("44 medicines in L04AC Interleukin Inhibitors, 33 of them currently authorized."); a choice of statuses counts as one more filter (`?status=Withdrawn`: "363 medicines match these filters, none of them currently authorized."). A = currently authorized (`isAuthorizedNow()`: status Authorized with an approval date). Never "authorized today" (phase 4c, user feedback: it reads as "got its authorization today"; `labels.test.js` rejects it). With a status filter other than the default the dek starts with the medicines by status (`statusBreakdown()`, `UI.headline.statuses()`: "By status: 1,573 authorized (6 without an approval date, not counted above), 363 withdrawn, 268 applications withdrawn, 64 refused and 83 with other statuses."; the parenthesis since the phase 4c review, as 1,573 next to the headline's 1,567 looked like an error); then substances and types. **Top row** (owner decisions 2026-09-29: "2x2 matrix", then revised the same day: the chart full width, the tiles under it; `.top-row-frame` in `index.html`, `tiles.js`): "Authorized over time" (authorized series as before; forced colors: its legend's line keys keep the series colours, as the lines do, audit 2026-09-30, S3; authorization history, so neither the status filter (owner decision 2026-09-29: a medicine withdrawn in 2020 was authorized in 2015) nor the year filter (shaded) applies: `filterProducts(…, OVER_TIME_EXCEPT)` in `filters.js`, a real-data test checks that under the default its series is the pipeline's; its subtitle says so, `UI.overTime.subtitle`: "Authorized products and distinct active substances at each month end, those withdrawn since included. Every filter applies but the status and the approval years, which are shaded.") is the dashboard's first card, across the main column (the chart follows its width: ResizeObserver as before), and under it the four type tiles (Orphan, Generic, Biosimilar and Advanced therapy (Orphan, no type, first, then the types in `TYPE_ORDER`, design sweep 2026-10-01, B5) with their share of N, a meter and their caption, shown at every width; explained on hover, focus and tap as before, `tabindex="0"`), rows as tall as their tiles (the figure 1.5rem). The frame is a size container (the main column's width); as many tiles to a row as keep the widest badge whole, a tile needing about 158px ("Advanced therapy", 124px in Geist, plus 2 x 16px padding and the border; measured 2026-09-29): four in a row from 672px (4 x 158 + 3 gaps of 12px = 668, rounded up), 2 x 2 from 328px, else one per row. Measured (main column → tile width): four in a row at 1440px with the default 320px sidebar (1,041 → 251px) and the widest, 560px (801 → 191px), and on a 768px tablet (721 → 171px); 2 x 2 at 1024px with the default sidebar (625 → 306px; the narrowest, 260px: 685 → 162px, four in a row), on a 390px phone (358 → 173px) and a 360px one (328 → 158px, the badge taking 0.3px of the padding); one per row on 320px phones (288px). The "Medicines" (every status) and "Currently authorized" tiles are gone (owner decision 2026-09-29: the headline states both numbers). Under the top row the Union Register note ("{n} of the medicines EMA lists as currently authorized are no longer authorized according to the EU Union Register.") and "{n} authorized medicines without an approval date are left out of “Authorized over time” and of the currently authorized count." (hidden at 0; owner decision 2026-09-29: the default headline counts them). Order in the main column: the page header (heading, lead, dek, filter chips), then the tabs (F · Spacious, phase 2; see "Tabs"): Overview (top row, notes, previews), Protection ("Estimated protection ending"), Classes and areas (breakdown card, the conditions card (always, since phase 4c)), Companies ("Who is active where"), By year ("Approvals per year", a card, its brush in sync with the approval year slider), Medicines (table). Table: every matching medicine, newest approval first, undated ones last by status (`STATUS_ORDER` in `approvals.js`) then name (`newestFirst()`); the caption says so (`UI.table.caption()`).
- **Who is active where** (F · Spacious, phase 3: the card leads with a takeaway and its captions and notes are in its (i) panel; see "Card polish") (phase 4a, `activity.js`, `holderActivity()` / `keyCounts()` / `topKeys()` in `facets.js`): a real `<table>` (named by the card title, described by the subtitle above the scroll area) of the 15 company groups with the most matching medicines (rows; companies part 2: `holderActivity(…, holderOf, labelOf)` by `group_key`, medicines without a holder in none; `<th scope="row">` buttons: filter `mah` = [group]; the group's badge, name, then its matching medicines, named with the EMA holder names behind them: "Roche, 40 medicines: Roche Registration GmbH, …", `UI.activity.holderRow()`, the names also in the tooltip above "Show only …"; after the button its company page link, `.cond-link`; design sweep A5, 2026-10-01: badge, name and total on one baseline, that line centred in the button, so a one-line row's text sits on its heat cells' centre line (4.5px above it before; a two-line name's block is centred, its first line still about 10px above), and starting on the card's text edge (145px at 1440, 33px at 390; 4px inside it before): no left padding, the table's 2px border spacing taken back by its margin, the scroll box reaching 4px into the card's padding so the focus ring shows, and the sticky column at `left: -4px`, so scrolled sideways it covers that padding, 4px left of where it rests) by columns chosen with a segmented toggle ("Columns", in the card's View options since F · Spacious, phase 2; UI state, not in the URL): "ATC groups" (level-1 groups as code badges; with exactly one ATC code, its children level as in the breakdown drill, a leaf itself: `atcClassesAt()` in `atc.js`) or "Therapeutic areas" (the top 12 MeSH branches by count, never the categories (owner decision 2026-09-29, `tree.branches`), then a static "Other" for the rest; phase 4f: with exactly one area selected, the top 12 of the areas one level below it in the tree (a category: its branches, "Columns: areas in [Diseases ×]"), a leaf itself, and that area is the toggle above the table, "Columns: areas in [Neoplasms ×]", as the ATC class; phase 4g: for a branch with root tags, then, as the breakdown's static row, a static last column "Tagged only as Neoplasms or Cancer", the medicines tagged only with them, which no child column holds; not for a node, where it mostly repeated each holder's total, phase 4g review). Column header buttons filter `atc` = [code] or `area` = the area's `canonical()` keys (an ATC column's tip, and the class toggle's, led by the class's explanation: see "ATC class explanations"). A cell = the holder's matching medicines in that column (a medicine can count in several); non-empty cells are buttons ("Novartis Europharm Limited, L Antineoplastic and Immunomodulating Agents: 30 medicines", `UI.activity.cell()`) that set `mah` and `atc` or `area` (those two dimensions replaced). Row headers, column headers and cells are toggles (phase 4c, user request): `aria-pressed` when their filter is exactly the current one (`patchIsSet()` in `url.js`), and a click then clears what they set (`togglePatch()`: Novartis again shows every holder). Sorting (phase 4c, UI state; its own header row since the phase 4c review, so a column header's name stays the column's): under Holder "Name" (A–Z first) and "Total" (most first; the default, pressed when no other sort is: the order is always visible), an arrow under each column ("Sort holders by {column}": most in that column first). Phase 4f (user decision): a second click on the sort in force reverses it (counts fewest first, names Z–A; `nextSort()`, `defaultSortDirection()` in `facets.js`), another sort starts in its first direction; ties keep their order either way (by total, then name; `sortActivityRows(rows, sort, direction)`); each button's arrow shows the order it sorts in (up: fewest first or A–Z), the pressed one's name adds it ("Sort holders by name, Z to A", `UI.sortOrder.name()`), and the sorted header has `aria-sort` "ascending" / "descending" (Holder for name and total, whose cells show the total after the name; a column for its sort); a column sort whose column is gone falls back to the total (most first), and switching columns resets it. Filter toggles say what a click does: tooltip "Show only {name}" ("Shown alone: click again to clear" when pressed) and a shared description "Filters the dashboard; select again to clear." (`aria-describedby`). With one ATC class shown by its child classes (a column or cell click drills in), that class is a pressed toggle above the table ("Columns: classes in [L Antineoplastic … ×]"; the column's focus key, so focus lands on it after the drill) whose click clears the ATC filter again. Touch screens and phones get the ATC columns' names under the table (`#activity-legend`, aria-hidden: the headers' tooltips never show there). "Column order" (a segmented toggle, per column mode; in the card's View options): ATC groups by "Code" (default) or "Count", areas by "Name" or "Count" (default); a second click on the pressed one reverses it (phase 4f; ties by key; the arrow after the text and the pressed button's name show the order: "Count, fewest first"); Other stays last (`orderActivityColumns(columns, counts, order, by, direction)`). Sort buttons are 24px, 44px on phones; pressed buttons carry the accent wash with the segmented control's underline, a pressed cell a ring and a bold underlined number (forced colors: an outline); five shades of the accent (`--heat-1…5` from `--raised` to `--accent`, each with its number colour `--heat-{n}-text`, in `palette.test.js`), empty for 0. The table scrolls sideways inside the card (`.activity-scroll`) with the holder column sticky, so the page never does; its `scroll-padding-inline-start` (the holder column's width + 4px, CSSOM) keeps a focused cell clear of that column (WCAG 2.4.11); 44px buttons on phones; on phones the holder column is 7.5rem (bug hunt 2026-10-01: 203px, so at 320px about one cell showed beside it; now 2 cells and part of a third at 320px, 4 at 390 and 412): the badge and the total on a line, the name wrapped under them (rows 46px, 65px for a two-line name), the company page link 28px wide and 44px tall, its 24px outlined box drawn by its icon; rebuilt on every render, focus goes back to the same control (else the table's first button). Note (companies part 2): "Companies by current owner as of {date}; each row lists the holder names EMA publishes in its tooltip." (`UI.activity.note()`, the groups' curation date).
- **Layout (F · Spacious, phase 1; replaced redesign phase 3's sidebar layout)**: no sidebar at any width. A top bar across the page (see "F · Spacious redesign"), then the main column, centred, 1200px at most with 32px at its sides (16px on phones; `main` and `footer` `max-width: 1264px`): the Try line and the intro card (on the untouched overview), a lookup result, then the page header (heading, the answer headline as its lead, the dek, Download CSV; then the filter chips), then the cards (24px apart, 24px padding; since phase 2 on tabs under the page header, see "Tabs"). The facet sections (static markup in `index.html`, `#facet-{type,modality,atc,area,mah,status,years}`) live hidden in `#facet-store` and are borrowed by a chip's popover (desktop) or sheet (below 1024px) while it is open: medicine type (all four, stack order), modality (the tree, once its data has loaded), ATC (the tree with its search), therapeutic area (the tree with its search, phase 4f; see "Therapeutic area tree"), company (the company tree with its search, companies part 2; see "Companies"), status (all present, by count, each with its status-hue dot; Authorized checked by default, owner decision 2026-09-29; under the rows a text button, `#status-widen` in `facet-panel.js`: "Include withdrawn, refused and pending" (`UI.facets.statusWiden`) sets every status, and with any other choice "Authorized only" (`UI.facets.statusDefault`) goes back to the default; unchecking the one checked row leaves none checked, every status, as in every facet, never an empty dashboard; the Status chip's popover and sheet show it), approval year (see "Approval year filter"). The sidebar (`#facets`, its head with "Filters", the active count and "Reset all", its note), its resizable splitter (`sidebar-resize.js`, `--sidebar-w`, localStorage `approval-atlas:sidebar-width`), the collapsible section headings (`facet-sections.js`, localStorage `approval-atlas:facets-open`, the summaries after collapsed titles) and `--header-h` are gone (old localStorage keys are left as they are, unused). Rows are real checkboxes in `<label>`s (32px desktop, 48px in sheets) whose text includes the count ("Biosimilar, 150"); checked rows sit on the accent wash (never muted, even at count 0, in the trees too: `facet-tree.js` marks only unchecked rows `.empty`); rows are keyed, so focus survives re-renders, and a row unchecked there stays listed (`facetRows()` `keep`) until that section's search or "Show …" changes, so its focus is not lost. After "Show …" adds rows, focus moves to the first new row. `facet-panel.js` renders the type and status lists wherever their sections are (`createFacetPanel({ onChange })`).
- **Filter chips** (F · Spacious, phase 1, owner decision: a chip bar above the content in place of the filter sentence, its "All filters" button and the sidebar; `filter-bar.js`, `facets.js` `filterChips()` / `activeFilterCount()`, copy `UI.filters`): under the page header's heading and lead, `#filter-chips` in a `.filter-bar` group named "Filters": one chip per dimension in a fixed order (Medicine type, Modality (only once the modality data has loaded: `lookups.modalityNames`), ATC class, Therapeutic area, Company, Status, Approval year; fixed, so a chip does not move when it changes, unlike the mockup's active-last order). Inactive: a dashed `--field-border` pill (3:1) with a circled plus, "⊕ Medicine type", `aria-haspopup="dialog"`, `aria-expanded` while its popover is open. Active: an accent-wash pill with an accent hairline, "{name} | {value}" (the "|" aria-hidden, a visually hidden ": ", so it reads "Medicine type: Biosimilar"), then a remove button ("Remove medicine type filter: Biosimilar", `UI.filters.remove()`, which clears its keys: `chip.clears`). The Status chip (owner decision 2026-09-29, "Authorized by default"; merged from the filter sentence of #22): inactive, "⊕ Status", in the default state (Authorised, no `status` key), and not counted in "N active filters" (url.js `filterIsSet()` / `activeFilterCount()`, the one count, which the headline uses too); widened it is active, "Status | Every status" (`?status=all`, `UI.facets.everyStatus`), "Status | Withdrawn" or "Status | 2 selected", and its × (and Clear, and Clear filters) returns to the default (`DEFAULT_STATE.status`). The sentence's own wording for it (`UI.sentence.statusDefault` "authorized status", `defaults.status` "every status") stays in `UI.sentence` for `tokenLabel()`, unused by the chips. Which dimensions are active, and the one value's explanation (type, status, modality: `data-tip` on the pill, `aria-describedby` on its button), come from the filter tokens (`sentenceParts()`, kept for this, so the chips follow whatever the tokens say is a filter); the value is `sectionSummary()`: one value named as the token names it (a status by its label alone, "Authorized"; "L04AC Interleukin Inhibitors"; "Diseases"; a root tag "tagged Cancer"), several rows of one company by that company ("Mylan S.A.S."), else "{n} selected" (two ATC classes are one chip, "2 selected"), the years as the slider shows them ("2015–2026", one year "2024"). After the chips while any filter is active: "[2] active filters · Clear filters" (`#filter-summary`: the count in an accent badge, `UI.filters.active()`; Clear filters clears every filter key, the years too, and focus goes to the first chip). Rebuilt on every render; focus goes back to the same control (a removed chip's open button). A chip opens its section: from 1024px in a popover under it (`popover.js`, see "F · Spacious redesign"), below that in a bottom sheet (see "Sheets"). Phones (≤ 720px): the chips one row that scrolls sideways (44px tall, remove buttons 44×44, 4px above and below so focus rings are not clipped, an edge fading (CSS mask) where more chips lie beyond it: `edgeFade()` in `filter-bar.js`, pure and tested, sets `.scroll-start` / `.scroll-end`; an active one shows its value alone, "Authorized", its name visually hidden, a value wider than the pill cut with an ellipsis inside it, the button's name keeping it whole: bug hunt 2026-10-01, "H Systemic Hormonal Preparations, …" ran over its ×, whose tap then opened the sheet), the count and Clear filters under it. `#filter-result` announces the new headline after filter edits (debounced) as before. The sentence's copy that only it used (`UI.sentence.tokenName`, `remove`, `dimensions`, `reset`, `allFilters`) and `filter-sentence.js` are gone; `UI.sentence` keeps the tokens' words, defaults and counts.
- **Approval year filter** (`#facet-years`, `year-slider.js` `createYearStrip()`, copy `UI.yearStrip`; F · Spacious, phase 1: the Approval year chip's popover on desktop, its sheet below 1024px; before, the sidebar's last collapsible section): the section holds a slim one-colour histogram (phase 4f, user decision: the status stacks, their legend and the undated note moved to "Approvals per year", Stack by Status, the one stacked per-year chart; `yearHistogram()` in `facets.js`: the medicines with an approval date per year by the facet rule, every filter but the years, one bar per year of the data range, `{ year, count }`; bars in the accent (`--bar`) inside the range, `--raised` with a `--field-border` outline outside (3:1, `palette.test.js`); `<title>` "{year}: {n} approvals" (`UI.years.tooltipTitle()`); the SVG is aria-hidden, `UI.yearStrip.summary()` is read instead; 36px bars desktop, 28px phone, half the phase 4a height; ticks every 5 or 10 years by width: every 10 below about 290px, every 5 above), the range as text (start left, end right; data bounds when open-ended), then a two-thumb slider (its Reset button is gone: the popover's and sheet's Clear reset the years). It takes the popover's width (380px) or a sheet's; hidden in the store it draws no bars (no width), and the ResizeObserver on `#year-hist` re-renders when it moves into a popover or sheet. The popover focuses the Start thumb (`FOCUS_TARGETS` in `main.js`). The slider: two native `<input type="range">` (min = first data year, max = last, step 1; "Start year" / "End year", `aria-valuetext` the year) over one track (`--field-border` outline); the thumbs take the pointer, and a click on the track moves the nearest thumb there and focuses it (`yearAt()` inverts `thumbCenter()`; `nearestThumb()`: the closer one, at a tie or on the same year Start below the range, else End — so coinciding thumbs are never stuck; WCAG 2.5.7 single-pointer alternative); a click on a year's bar (its whole column) shows only that year (from = to), and a click on the bar of the one selected year shows all years again (`toggleYear()`, phase 4a); the bars stay non-focusable: the slider is the keyboard path; each bar sits at its year's thumb centre (`thumbCenter()`: thumb / 2 + fraction × (width − thumb); `--thumb` in CSS, read by JS). Thumbs 24px desktop, 28px visible in a 44px hit area on phones and in sheets (`.sheet .year-slider`, tablets too; `--thumb-edge` transparent rim), surface fill with a 2px accent ring, focus ring on the thumb on any `:focus` (the popover opening focuses it without `:focus-visible`); forced-colors styles (bars `CanvasText` inside the range, `Canvas` with a `GrayText` outline outside). The moved thumb stops at the other (`thumbRange()`), where they overlap the one that can move away is on top (`startOnTop()`); a thumb at a data bound leaves that end open (null, as `normalizeYearRange()`). Dragging updates thumbs, text and bar colours at once; the filter applies on release (`change`, or `pointerup` for a drag back to its start), not mid-drag, as a render per step would redraw the dashboard and the bars above the thumbs; keys and clicks apply at once: arrows ±1, PageUp/PageDown ±5 (browsers step 10%), Home/End. The `#filter-result` announcement stays debounced (500 ms after the last update). The "Approvals per year" card keeps its brush (drag: a range; a click without a drag, press and release under 3px apart: the same `toggleYear()` on the clicked column, `yearAtPixel()`; a finger's first tap on a year shows its numbers, a second sets the filter (see "Type explanations", the charts' tooltips); pointer only, its keyboard path is the slider too; the cards above it re-render with other heights, so the chart is kept at its screen position: `keepInPlace()` / `scrollAnchor` in `main.js`); brush and slider both read/write `state.from/to` and re-derive from state on every render, so they stay in sync (the slider skips syncing its thumbs during a drag).
- **Sheets** (`sheet.js`; below 1024px): one native `<dialog>` opened with `showModal()` (focus trap, Escape, inert page; a backdrop click closes it; a visible 44px × button, "Close filters", after Clear since the phase 4c review; no grab handle, as the sheet does not swipe). A filter chip opens it with its one section (F · Spacious, phase 1; the sentence's tokens and "All filters", which opened every section, are gone), borrowed from the hidden store (a comment marker keeps its place) and returned on close, so controls and state are shared; its title is the chip's name ("ATC class", `UI.filters.names`); Clear resets the chip's keys; filters apply live, the footer button "Show {N} medicines" (the matching medicines) closes it, and focus returns to the chip (looked up again by its focus key, as chips are rebuilt). Focus lands on the sheet title (a search field would open the phone keyboard); a focused search scrolls to the top of the sheet body so its rows show below it. Only the body scrolls (`.sheet { overflow: hidden }`, `.sheet-body { position: relative }` contains the rows' visually hidden text). A link followed from it (a condition or company page) closes it without handing focus back to the chip; so does Back or Forward (navigation fixes 2026-10-01: it stayed open over the view shown, and edited the overview hidden behind it), focus then going to the view's heading when in view, else to the page (see "Scroll"). Filter announcements go to the sheet's own status while it is open (inert live regions are not read). On a touch screen a tapped row's tip shows in a strip above the foot, covering no row (see "MeSH explainers"). The sheet closes when the viewport reaches 1024px (a popover closes below it). Animation only without `prefers-reduced-motion`.
- **Class and filtered views**: breakdown bars count every matching medicine ("Medicines by ATC level 1"); ATC bars are stacked by medicine type (`typeSplit()`, type mids, 1px gaps, `TYPE_ORDER` left to right, one legend per card of the types present); each bar's name adds the split ("L04AC05 Ustekinumab, 12 medicines: 1 Other, 11 Biosimilar"); each bar is explained as its tree row (`atcClassTip()`: the class's explanation, then its level; not on a touch screen, where a tap drills; see "ATC class explanations"). The therapeutic area mode drills like ATC (phase 4f, `areaBreakdown()` in `main.js`, `areaBreakdownRows()`): without one area selected, the MeSH branches, never the categories (owner decision 2026-09-29: the charts start at the branches; `tree.branches`) (top 20, then Other; with several areas selected the branches holding one, or under a selected category, are marked toggles: a marked branch removes the areas that mark it); a bar opens its area alone (`area` = its `canonical()` keys), whose bars are the areas one level below it (most first, top 20, then Other; one category selected: its branches), then "tagged at this level" (the medicines tagged with the area itself; a branch's: "Tagged only as Neoplasms or Cancer", the medicines tagged only with its root tags, phase 4g; none for a category); a leaf shows only itself; "Up one level" (a term: to its first parent in tree order; a branch: to its category when the branch was opened from the category's bars or view, else to all areas, `areaUpLevel()`, `areaDrillVia()`: UI state followed on every render, owner decision 2026-09-29) and the path "All therapeutic areas › Diseases › Neoplasms › …" (through the category; `renderAreaPath()` in `area-tree.js`) above, titles "Medicines in {name} by therapeutic area" / "Medicines in {name}"; each bar ends with its condition page link. The company mode drills too (companies part 2, `companyBreakdown()` in `main.js`, `companyBreakdownRows()`): the company groups (top 20, then Other; with several values selected the groups holding one are marked toggles), a bar opens its value alone (`mah` = its `canonical()`), whose bars are its companies (a group whose one company has its name: its EMA holder names), a company's its EMA holder names, then a static "No EMA holder name" row (the medicines EMA names no holder for; no link or badge), a leaf only itself; each bar's key is its tree row's filter value (`rowValue()`), so Organon's "Merck Sharp & Dohme B.V." bar opens that 1 medicine, not the company's 63; "Up one level", the path "All companies › [RO] Roche › …" (`renderCompanyPath()` in `company-tree.js`), titles "Medicines of {name} by company" / "… by EMA holder name" / "Medicines of {name}"; groups carry their badge, every group and company bar its company page link, and each bar's name and tooltip list the EMA holder names behind it (the first 8, then how many more; `UI.companies.barLabel()`, `named()`); "{n} medicines without a holder are not shown." Area and company bars stay one accent colour. Bar rows (the breakdown's and the protection card's) put their labels and values on the card's text edges, their 6px padding and hover wash reaching into the card's padding (`margin-inline: -6px`; a row followed by its page link keeps its right margin; design sweep A9, 2026-10-01: 151 and 1289 against the card's 145 and 1295 at 1440); the lookup pages' "Show all statuses" checkbox has no left margin, on the page's edge (124 against 120 before). That row looks as a filter's checkbox row since the design sweep's B7 (2026-10-01; `.toggle-all`, the `.facet-row` look: a 16px checkbox without the browser's margin, 2px 6px padding and a -6px margin, so the checkbox stays on the edge, 32px tall, the wash on hover and the accent wash while checked; phones 44px with a 20px checkbox, as a sheet's rows; it was a native 13px checkbox in a 24px label). A "Sort" segmented toggle in the card's View options (under the card head until F · Spacious, phase 2) (phase 4c, UI state kept across modes and renders): "Count" (default, most first) or "Code" (ATC, code order) / "MeSH" (areas in MeSH tree order, as the tree: each row's `rank` from `areaBreakdownRows()`; owner request 2026-09-28, it was by name) / "Name" (companies, A–Z; all still the top 20 by count); a second click on the pressed one reverses it (phase 4f: fewest first, ties in their order; Z–A; tree order reversed), an arrow after its text shows the order and the pressed button's name says it ("Count, most first", "MeSH, tree order": `UI.sortOrder.tree`, `renderSortButton(…, kind)`); Other and the "code incomplete" / "not more specific" rows stay last (`sortBreakdownRows(rows, order, by, direction)` in `approvals.js`). Area bars carry their MeSH explainer (see "MeSH explainers").
- **Estimated protection ending** (titled "Protection ending (est.)" since the copy review, applied in the review of phase 3) (F · Spacious, phase 3: the card leads with a takeaway and its captions and notes are in its (i) panel; see "Card polish") (2026-09-29, kept by the owner the same day; scrutiny "Loss-of-exclusivity calendar"; frontend only, no R change; `protection-calendar.js` pure and tested, `protection-calendar-card.js`, `UI.protectionCalendar`): a card after "Approvals per year" over the currently authorized medicines matching the filters whose estimate `status` is `protected`, one toggle button per year (this data year and the next four, then "{year} or later"), counted by `market_protection_end_min`'s year; copies (`basis` `follows_reference` or `reference_not_found`) are never counted in the years or the unclear line (review 2026-09-29: Palbociclib Viatris sat in 2026 beside Ibrance, one loss of exclusivity counted twice); a copy with its own orphan market exclusivity running after today is in the orphan-only list, "No market protection of its own (a copy)" (morning QA 2026-09-29: Hyftor, Kinpeygo); each bar split into the medicines without and with orphan market exclusivity running past `market_protection_end_max` (`--bar`, `--pink-mid`, ≥ 1.5:1 in lightness, `palette.test.js`; the orphan count also as text under the year, "4 orphan"); bars to the scale of the busiest single year, the later bar drawn broken when longer (`barShares()`); `unclear` estimates a muted line of their own, never in the bars ("{n} more medicines may lose market protection by {latest year}: …"); ended or unclear estimates whose orphan market exclusivity still runs after today and after `market_protection_end_max` (Soliris, Vyndaqel, Hyftor…; `orphanOnly`, not in the unclear count) a line whose "List them" toggle lists them by orphan end. A year (UI state, not in the URL) lists its medicines below, by date (heading "Earliest estimated end of market protection in {year}"; name to its card, company badge and group, substances, "Market protection ends (est.) {min} – {max}", orphan exclusivity when later; 20, then "Show all"; links keep focus across re-renders, `data-focus-key` `med-`/`co-{number}`; on phones the medicine link is 24px tall (padded, a negative margin keeping the line; it was 21px) and a company wrapped under it starts 16px below (`.pc-item-head` row gap), so the company link's 44px target (13px above its line) never reaches it: 2026-09-30, axe target-size 110 → 0 across every year's list and the orphan-only list, all rows shown, at 320 and 390); orphan ends say "(est.)" unless `end_source` is `register`. **Exact or estimated** (owner decision 2026-09-30, "be specific where you can, else estimate": the 5-year horizon stays; exact dates plain, computed ones "(est.)"): approval dates and `counted_from` are real approvals, never marked; data exclusivity and market protection ends are computed, always "(est.)" (on a copy's lead too: "Ibrance's protection until 2026–2027 (est.)"); an orphan market exclusivity end is exact when `end_source` is `register` (the Union Register's EndDate, which R prefers to a computed one, and the frontend on a same-day tie: `byOrphanEnd()` in `protection.js`), else "(est.)" (link date + 10 years + extension). A label over several orphan ends (the legend, a year button's name, the orphan-only line and list heading, the Protection takeaway) is marked by their sources: "(est.)" when all are computed, none when all are the register's, "(partly est.)" when they mix (`orphanEstimateOf()` in `labels.js`; `calendarBuckets()` keeps each year's `orphanEnds`, `orphanEndsOf()`); each listed medicine's own end keeps its own mark. On 2026-09-29 every running orphan end is computed (the register fills EndDate only once exclusivity has ended): the bars' 40 medicines with orphan market exclusivity running later, the 26 orphan-only ones and the 161 card leads naming one all read "(est.)"; the exact register ends (153 rows, 121 medicines) are all ended ones, shown in the card's More details as "ended {date} (Union Register)" (Soliris and Vyndaqel show both kinds). The caption repeats the medicine card's caveats (earlier national authorizations, the possible extra year, copies not yet checked by hand). The protection dataset loads when the card nears the viewport (its tab shown) or the page is idle ("Loading estimates…" until then). The company page adds "Protection ending (est.)": its currently authorized protected medicines by end year and orphan-only ones by orphan end year ("orphan market exclusivity (est.) only"), unclear ones counted; on phones each medicine link 44px tall, so each line of the list is (bug hunt 2026-10-01: 21px, side by side 8px apart; Roche's list 234 → 352px). Estimates only, never "patent".
- **Approvals per year** (F · Spacious, phase 3: the card leads with a takeaway and its captions and notes are in its (i) panel; see "Card polish") (review of phase 3, owner calls 2026-09-30: titled "Medicines by year of approval" (`UI.years.title`); stacked by Status its note is "Each medicine once, in the year it was first approved; colors show its status today." (`UI.years.noteStatus`) and its legend is headed "Status today" before "Bottom to top:" (`renderStackLegend(…, heading)`); the other modes keep "Bottom to top:") (phase 4c, user request; `chart.js`, `yearStacks()` / `topWithOther()` in `facets.js`): "Stack by: Medicine type | ATC | Company | Status | Modality" (Modality: see "Modality") (UI state; in the card's View options since F · Spacious, phase 2). Status only with the status filter widened (owner decision 2026-09-30; every status or a choice of statuses: `statusStackAvailable()` in `facets.js`, tested): under the default (authorized only) it would be one series, so its button is hidden, and the panel says, on a line of its own under the controls, "Include withdrawn, refused and pending to stack by status." (`#chart-stack-hint`, `UI.years.stack.statusHint`; the words before " to" a `.lead-button`, described by the rest, which widens the status filter (`?status=all`), stacks by status and moves focus to the Status button); a Status stack falls back to Medicine type when the status filter returns to the default (`yearStackMode()`, applied on every render of the card). Links without a `status` key cannot be told from the default, so they stay authorized-only (see "URL keys"). Medicine type in the type mids, bottom to top in `TYPE_ORDER` (Other, Generic, Biosimilar, Advanced therapy: the one display order of the types, as the breakdown's bars and legend, the type filter, the tiles and the dek; design sweep 2026-10-01, B5: it was Advanced therapy at the bottom, `MEDICINE_TYPES`, which is precedence, not display order). Status (phase 4f, user decision; the approval-years strip's stacks of phase 4a): the statuses present in `STATUS_ORDER`, Authorized at the bottom in `--status-authorized`, the others in their status hue's mid (`statusColor()`), each legend entry explained on hover and tap (`UI.statusTips`); counting "each medicine counted once, by its current status"; below the chart "{n} medicines without an approval date (refused, application withdrawn, pending…) are not in this chart" (+ ", and the year filter leaves them out of every count" with a year filter; `UI.years.undatedStatuses()`), or, under the default status filter (owner decision 2026-09-29: only Authorized then, its undated ones authorized ones), the plain "{n} medicines without an approval date are not shown." (`UI.years.undated()`). The chart follows the status filter (by default the medicines still authorized, by approval year). ATC: the 6 level-1 groups with the most matching medicines (phase 4c review: 14 neighbouring hues could not be matched to the legend), in code order, each in its group's hue mid (each legend entry explained by the class's explanation, levels 1–4: see "ATC class explanations"), then "Other classes"; with exactly one ATC code (`drillCode()`), its child classes (`atcClassesAt()`), the top 8 in code order in `STACK_HUES` mids, then "Other classes" on top; Other (both modes and holders) is low-key: the `--raised` fill with a 1px `--field-border` outline (legend swatch and tooltip key too), so the named series carry the colour (it was a slate mid, most of the chart's ink in Holder mode); the note names the top ones only when there is an Other ("…: the 6 classes with the most matching medicines, the rest as Other classes"); a medicine counts once in every class it has (the note says so), so a column can be taller than its medicines. Company (companies part 2): the 8 company groups with the most matching dated medicines (most at the bottom) in their group's colours (`companySeriesColors(keys, palette)` in `badges.js`, review of companies part 2: brand and damped mids can be near twins, Teva and Pfizer, Roche and AbbVie: each group, most medicines first, takes the first of its hue's mid, its text shade, then the mids and text shades of the other damped hues, nearest to its own first, that differs from every colour taken before by `SERIES_DISTANCE` (OKLab 0.06) in light and in dark mode; the palette of both modes is read once from the page's style sheets, `readPalette()` in `main.js`; `palette.test.js` checks the real top-8 sets, overall and per ATC group; the legend keeps the group's badge in its brand colour beside the swatch), legend entries with the group's badge and, as their explanation (`data-tip`), the EMA holder names behind them, then "Other companies" on top. **Same yearly totals in every mode** (user decision 2026-09-28: 2025 read 109 by Company but 107 by ATC, as Vimkunya and Wayrilz have no ATC code): the dated medicines a mode cannot place are one segment on top of each column, after Other, "No ATC code", "Coded only as {code}" (one ATC class selected: its medicines without a more specific code) or "No company" (`UI.years.unplaced`; `withUnplaced()` in `facets.js`, key `UNPLACED_KEY`, applied after `topWithOther()`; only when a dated medicine has none, tested with the per-year totals of every mode); low-key as Other and told apart by `--field-border` hatching on the `--raised` fill (`STACK_UNPLACED` in `main.js`: an SVG pattern in the chart's `<defs>`, a `repeating-linear-gradient` in the legend swatch, a dashed tooltip key), in the legend (last) and the tooltip, so "{year}: {n} approvals" is the same in every mode (the notes that named these medicines below the chart are gone). (Before: the 8 holders in `STACK_HUES` mids, still used for child ATC classes.) 1px gaps between segments in every mode. Legend: "Bottom to top:" then the series in stack order (forced colors: swatches kept with a border). Tooltip (at the pointer, following it: see "Type explanations"): "{year}: {n} approvals" (medicines, each once) then the non-empty series top to bottom. The summary (`UI.years.summary()`, the SVG's name) and the note (`UI.years.note()`, `UI.years.counting`) follow the mode; the note also says "EMA's annual reports count CHMP opinions instead, so their yearly totals differ." (step 4, #17: journalists quote EMA's figures, counted by opinion year); brush, click-a-year toggle and grey out-of-range columns as before.
- **Conditions card** (F · Spacious, phase 3: the card leads with a takeaway and its captions and notes are in its (i) panel; see "Card polish") (after "Estimated protection ending", as "Authorized over time" is the top row's since 2026-09-29; redesign 2026-09-29, owner-approved, of the "Most common conditions" list; `conditions-card.js`, the rows `conditionRows()` and what the card shows `conditionsCardState()` in `facets.js`, pure and tested on fixtures and the real data; copy `UI.conditions`; the company page's own "Most common conditions" section is unchanged): always shown (phase 4c, user feedback "doesn't always show?"). One row per specific condition (broad categories left out: see below) of the medicines shown (every medicine without a filter; phase 4f: with a therapeutic area filter only the conditions with a term within it, `conditionRows(…, { within })`, `inAreas()`), counted as the condition page it opens counts (review 2026-09-29: counting the EMA term alone, 182 of 659 rows disagreed with their page, e.g. Cancer 17 treatments against its page's 260, and fewest first opened with broad terms such as Infections, 1 treatment against its page's 158): a MeSH descriptor of an EMA term, its spellings one row named as its page (EMA's Cancer and Neoplasms: Neoplasms; Infection and Infections; 654 rows for 659 terms on 2026-09-28), its medicines those `buildConditions()` gives it (tagged with it or a narrower EMA term: the lookup's `need("conditions")` dataset, asked for when the lookup starts; "Loading…" until it arrives, "Not available right now." when it fails) among the medicines shown; a term without a descriptor counts alone. Each row: `count` (its medicines of every status), `authorized` (those with status Authorised, as its page's headline) and `treatments` (their distinct substance sets, as its page's dek, step 4 #10: `ema_substance_equivalents.json` spellings joined once loaded, a combination counting on its own; `setKeyOf`); tested on the real data for every row against its page (Arthritis, Rheumatoid 16 treatments, 47 of 64; Abdominal Neoplasms 4, 11 of 16; Neoplasms 260, 386 of 549; Infections 158, 213 of 351). **Specific conditions only** (owner decision 2026-09-29: so counted, most first opened with broad categories, Neoplasms 260, Immune System Diseases 202, Infections 158, Nutritional and Metabolic Diseases 153 …): a condition is broad when every MeSH tree number of its descriptor is at level 1 or 2 (a branch root tag: Neoplasms, Cancer; a level-2 number only: Lung Diseases, C08.381), specific with any at level 3 or deeper (Diabetes Mellitus: C18.452.394.750 as well as C19.246). Read from the area tree, at hand when the card first renders (`tree.broad()` in `areas.js`: a term with no level-3 node above or at it on any path, from `ema_therapeutic_area_branches.json` and `ema_therapeutic_area_subtree.json`), not from `mesh_descriptor_notes.json`, which loads later; a real-data test checks the rule gives the same conditions as every tree number in the notes, and the same rows once they have loaded. Broad ones are left out in both directions and within the selected areas (`conditionRows(…, { broad })`, which counts them in `broad`); the others keep their pages' counts. On 2026-09-28 40 of 654 conditions are left out: Neoplasms, Immune System Diseases, Infections, Nutritional and Metabolic Diseases, Metabolic Diseases, Genetic Diseases, Inborn, Respiratory Tract Diseases, Skin Diseases, Lung Diseases, Digestive System Diseases …, and some level-2 ones that read narrower (Graft vs Host Disease C20.452, Ocular Hypertension, Anxiety Disorders, Retinal Diseases, Corneal Diseases, Fractures, Bone). Most first (treatments, authorized, every status): Lymphoproliferative Disorders 67, 91 of 123; Metabolism, Inborn Errors 55, 69 of 85; Diabetes Mellitus 52, 120 of 169; Glucose Metabolism Disorders 49, 95 of 139; HIV Infections 40, 59 of 79; Carcinoma, Bronchogenic 37, 56 of 94; Diabetes Mellitus, Type 2 36, 61 of 87; Kidney Diseases 36, 53 of 66; Bacterial Infections 36, 51 of 85; Carcinoma 35, 48 of 63 (Breast Neoplasms 33, Psoriasis and Arthritis, Rheumatoid further down); fewest first: Acanthamoeba Keratitis, Achondroplasia, Acidosis, Renal Tubular, Acne Vulgaris, Acute Pain, Acute Radiation Syndrome, Adrenal Cortex Neoplasms, Adrenal Hyperplasia, Congenital, Adrenoleukodystrophy, Alcohol-Related Disorders (1 treatment each, by authorized medicines then A to Z). When every condition of the medicines shown is broad (`?area=Cancer`), a line instead of the table: "The one condition of these medicines is a broad category, not ranked here: see the therapeutic area filter." (`UI.conditions.onlyBroad()`, `conditionsCardState()` "broad"). A real `<table>` (`#conditions-table`, named by the card title through `aria-labelledby`, described by the subtitle; explicit table roles): header Condition | Treatments | Authorized medicines; each row a `<th scope="row">` with the condition (a link to `?cond=<MeSH descriptor>` named as the descriptor, plain text without one; its MeSH explainer once the notes have loaded; then its branch chips, of the term named as the descriptor, else its first by name, see "Branch chips"), its treatments, then its medicines: a bar whose full length is its medicines of every status, scaled to the largest shown, the authorized part in `--bar`, the rest in `--bar-rest` (a lighter accent shade: `--heat-4` light, `--heat-3` dark; a chart mark ≥ 3:1 and ≥ 1.5:1 in lightness from `--bar`, `palette.test.js`; forced colors: the authorized part `CanvasText`, the rest `Canvas` outlined in `CanvasText`), 1px apart, then the authorized number and, muted, "of 169" with, visually hidden, " medicines of every status" (`UI.conditions.of()`, `everyStatus()`; review 2026-09-29: "47 of 64" did not say what the 64 counts; the cell's tooltip "120 authorized of 169 medicines of every status", a data-tip as every explanation (see "Type explanations"; tabindex -1, so a tap shows it)); numbers tabular and right-aligned. Ranking (UI state, not in the URL, kept across renders): by treatments (default) or authorized medicines, most (default) or fewest first; ties by the other count in the same direction, then by name A to Z; fewest first lists only conditions with an authorized treatment (a condition with none is not the one with the fewest), saying how many it leaves out ("66 conditions without an authorized treatment are not listed.", `UI.conditions.unlisted()`; 68 before broad ones were left out), and keeps the header (its sort buttons) even when that leaves no row (review 2026-09-29: hiding it left no way back to most first). When no condition of the medicines shown has an authorized medicine (e.g. `?status=Withdrawn`), a line instead of the table: "None of the 251 conditions of these medicines has an authorized medicine, so they are not ranked." (276 before broad ones were left out) (`UI.conditions.noneAuthorized()`; review 2026-09-29: the rows read A to Z, all 0, under "the most treatments"); a chip whose filter leads there hands focus to the area filter. A sort button in each of those two headers (`.sort-button.sort-text`, the activity table's styles; `nextSort()`: the first click sorts most first, a second on the sort in force reverses it; its arrow shows the order; the pressed one named "Sort by treatments, fewest first", `UI.sortOrder.name()`; `aria-sort` on the sorted header, whose `aria-label` is the column's name, so its cells read "Treatments", not the button's name; forced colors: the pressed one outlined, as the activity table's). The title says the ranking (`UI.conditions.title()`: "Conditions with the most treatments", "… the fewest treatments", "… the most authorized medicines", "… the fewest authorized medicines"; a proposal for the owner); the subtitle what is counted, then where the broad categories are ("Conditions of all 2,351 medicines in the EMA data, each with its narrower ones; treatments are the active substances or combinations of the authorized ones. Broad categories such as Neoplasms are in the therapeutic area filter.", "Conditions of the {N} medicines matching the filters, within the selected areas, each with its narrower ones; …"; the owner's wording, as a longer "… filter instead." took a sixth line at 390; under the default status filter (owner decision 2026-09-29) "Conditions of all 1,573 authorized medicines, each with its narrower ones; treatments are their active substances or combinations. …", "Conditions of the {N} authorized medicines matching the filters, …", `subtitle(…, authorizedOnly)`, and each medicines cell the authorized number alone, no "of 169" (every medicine shown is authorized), its tooltip "120 authorized medicines" (`UI.conditions.authorizedTip()`); "all … medicines" by default or with every status, "matching the filters" with another filter or a choice of statuses); the old hint "Open a condition to see its approval timeline." is gone (the names are underlined links). The first 10 rows, then "Show 10 more" (`#conditions-more`, a text button, 24px on desktop; focus then goes to the first new row's link, else its chips); back to 10 when the sort or the filters (`FILTER_KEYS`) change. Rebuilt on every render; focus comes back to the same link (`cond:{row key}`: the descriptor, else the term) or sort button (`sort:{key}`), a chip to the same chip of the same condition, else as in the medicines table (that condition's "+n", the same chip of another condition, the area filter). No condition among the medicines shown: a line instead (`UI.conditions.empty()`). A size container (`.conditions-card`): from 520px one line per condition (rows 25px: a line of chips, `.term-branches` without its 2px margin here, and a hairline; header 28px), the bar 8rem, 4rem in a card under 800px (review 2026-09-29: at 1024 with the 320px sidebar the Condition column had 233px and long names wrapped their chips; now 297px), and in a card of 520 to 559px beside a desktop sidebar "of 169" visually hidden too (the bar's length, the tooltip and screen readers keep it); in a card under 520px (at 1024 beside a sidebar wider than about 440px), and on every phone (≤ 720px; review 2026-09-29: from about 586 to 720px the card is 520px or wider, its 27px one-line rows overlapped the phones' 44px condition links and chip rims, and a tap on HIV Infections' C01 chip opened the next row; the `@media` block repeats the `@container` one, `conditions-table.test.js` keeps them alike), each row takes two lines, the condition, then "67 treatments" and the bar with "91 of 123 authorized" (the units `aria-hidden`, shown there only; the numbers three digits wide, at most 120 authorized and 169 of every status, Diabetes Mellitus, on 2026-09-28 (386 and 549, Neoplasms, before broad ones were left out), and the treatments cell 6.25rem, so every row's bar has the same width and one scale), the header's Condition cell visually hidden and its two sort buttons a line of their own at the top (they are controls, so they stay visible); below 360px the buttons 4px apart (review 2026-09-29: at 320 they wrapped onto two lines) and "of 169" visually hidden (the bar 49px, not 25). Phones: the condition links 45px tall (padding, a negative margin keeps the line), the chips' and "+n"'s rims as elsewhere, the sort buttons and "Show 10 more" 44px. Measured 2026-09-29 after broad ones were left out, with Geist loaded (the `site/dist/assets` fonts after `npm run build`; 2026-09-28 data, no filter, 10 rows; desktop widths with a classic scrollbar, phone widths without): 1440 with the 320px sidebar 427px most first (the subtitle two lines, rows 25px, header 28px; 401px before, with a one-line subtitle; the owner's estimate was about 380: the rows cannot be lower than their chips), 456px fewest first (the "not listed" line); 1024 with the 320px sidebar 448px (all rows one line; the subtitle three lines), fewest first 498px (one row two lines: Adrenal Hyperplasia, Congenital); 1440 with a 560px sidebar 427px, fewest first 456px; 1024 with a 380px sidebar 469px (one row two lines: Lymphoproliferative Disorders), fewest first 540px (three); 1024 with a 460px sidebar, stacked, 806px (the header 31px), fewest first 835px; landscape phones at 640, 667 and 700 stacked, 817px (rows 58.5px), fewest first 846px; 390 859px (rows 58.5px, the header 51px, the subtitle five lines; 834px before, four), fewest first 909px; 320 967px (three rows 79.5px: Lymphoproliferative Disorders; Metabolism, Inborn Errors; Carcinoma, Bronchogenic; the subtitle six lines), fewest first 1,080px (six rows 79.5px, their chips under the name); no sideways scroll of the card at any of them. `elementFromPoint` at 320, 390, 640, 667 and 700 (most and fewest first): no point of a row's controls reaches another row's; within a row, as elsewhere, a fill (chip or "+n") stands above a link's padding and the rims give way to both (again at 320 and 390 with the specific conditions, most and fewest first: every point of a link's text and of a chip's or "+n"'s fill reaches it, no point of a row's links, chips or "+n" (padding and rims included) reaches another row's, and the sort buttons and "Show 10 more" are their own).
- Possible follow-up: an "Orphan" filter (the artboards show one; not a filter dimension yet).

## F · Spacious redesign (owner-approved mockups 2026-09-29; built in phases, each stops for review)
- **Why**: the desktop main column felt crammed beside the sidebar. Owner decisions: structure by tabs (phase 2), filters as a chip bar above the content, no status KPI cards; the static mockups (`F-Desktop-Overview`, `-Companies`, `-Medicines`, `F-Phone-Overview`, `-Filter-Sheet`, `-Medicines`, drawn in the E · Sage tokens) are the design reference. Phase 1 (the shell and the filters, `feature/redesign-f`): done; phase 4 (the medicine card in blocks, `feature/redesign-f4-card`): done (PR #27); phase 2 (tabs: Overview, Protection, Classes and areas, Companies, By year, Medicines; `feature/redesign-f2-tabs`): built, for review (see "Tabs"); phase 3 (card polish; `feature/redesign-f3-polish`): built, for review (see "Card polish").
- **Card polish** (phase 3; `takeaways.js` pure and tested, copy `UI.takeaways` and `UI.cardInfo`, `view-options.js` `setupCardInfo()`). **Takeaways** (Cognitive Load / Selective Attention): every dashboard card leads, under its title, with one sentence computed from the data it shows (the filters applied; `.card-takeaway`, 15px in the ink, hidden when empty), in place of its method description: Authorized over time "1,567 medicines authorized with an approval date, up 57 in the last 12 months." (`overTimeTakeaway()`: the series' last point and its change from the point nearest a year before, none within a shorter series; "No medicines authorized with an approval date", "1 medicine …", "down", "unchanged over the last 12 months"; review of phase 3: named "with an approval date", as the headline counts the 6 without one too, and with a status (other than the default) or year filter set it adds "The status filter does not apply to this history." / "The year filter …" / "The status and year filters do not …"); Protection "Of the 459 medicines with market protection running (est.), 103 may lose it by the end of 2028, 14 of them with orphan market exclusivity (est.) running later." (scoped so it stands alone, final round before merge; "The 1 medicine with market protection running (est.) may lose it by …") (`protectionTakeaway()`: of the medicines whose estimated market protection runs, those whose earliest end falls by the end of the data year + 2, and how many of those have orphan market exclusivity running later (review of phase 3: so "lose market protection" is not read as "open to copies"); "The 1 medicine with market protection running is not estimated to lose it by …", "None of the {n} … is estimated to lose it by …"; none while the files load, which the card asks for when shown (`lookup.need("protection")`: a direct link need not wait for the page to be idle); never "patent"); the breakdown "L Antineoplastic and Immunomodulating Agents has the most medicines (490)." (`breakdownTakeaway()`, whatever its mode or drill: the bar with the most, an ATC class with its code; ties "A and B have the most medicines (5 each).", "3 groups …"; one bar "A: 1 medicine, the only group here." (in ATC mode "class" and "classes": "the only class here", "3 classes have the most medicines (5 each)."); static rows (a class alone, code incomplete, not more specific, not classified) and Other are no group; none for a leaf; review of phase 3: every bar shown tied while Other holds more (the top 20 cap) "Several groups have the most medicines (1 each).", and none while several values are selected (the bars are then every group, the selected ones marked, and the largest can be unrelated to them)); the conditions card "Lymphoproliferative Disorders has the most treatments (67)." (`conditionsTakeaway()` of the card's default ranking, `conditionRanking()` in `main.js`, shared with the Overview preview, whatever the sort shown; ties counted over every condition ranked, not the rows shown (review of phase 3: `?mah=g.bio-thera-solutions`: 14 tie; final round before merge: more than two tied on 1 treatment says little, so no sentence); Who is active where "Novartis has the most medicines (77), 27 of them in L Antineoplastic and Immunomodulating Agents." (`activityTakeaway()` over every company (`holderActivity()` uncapped; the table shows 15): the company with the most, whatever the rows' sort, and its medicines in its largest column (not Other); "all of them in …", one medicine "in …"; companies tied: named without, "20 companies have the most medicines (2 each)."; one company alone (review of phase 3): "Intas: 71 medicines, 35 of them in L …"); Medicines by year of approval "Of the medicines shown, 108 were approved in 2025, 44 of them biosimilars." (`yearsTakeaway()` of the dated medicines the chart shows; review of phase 3: worded by the medicines shown, as by default they are the authorized ones (EMA approved 109 in 2025, one withdrawn since) and under `?status=Withdrawn` one; the year `takeawayYear()`: the last full year (the data year less 1) inside the year filter, else the filter's last year, "so far" for the data's own year (`?from=2015&to=2020`: 2020); "None of the medicines shown was approved in …", "none of them biosimilars", "all of them biosimilars", one "a biosimilar" / "not a biosimilar"); Medicines: its caption, the count, as before. Estimates say "(est.)"; U.S. spelling, no em-dashes. **Measure** (design sweep A3, 2026-10-01): prose is at most 70ch wide (the page dek 72ch, as the MeSH definition), so its lines run about 96-105 characters at 1440 where they ran 112-197: the takeaways, the (i) panels' notes, the Medicines caption, the Union Register and undated notes, the results timeline's caption, the result tables' snippets, About and the footer; the intro card's scope line by a right padding (`max(0px, 100% - 70ch)`), so its hairline still spans the card. The medicines table's expanded indication (a cell's text, which a max-width does not cap) still runs the table's width. **(i)** (owner plan: captions into (i) tips): a real disclosure button beside the title (`.info-button`: `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls` its panel, named "About" and the card's title ("About Medicines by year of approval": `aria-labelledby` a visually hidden lead in it and the title, review of phase 3), its tooltip "About this card"; open in forced colors: Highlight fill; a circled i, 28px round, 44px on phones; the accent while open), not a data-tip: it works the same on touch, keyboard and screen readers, and a method description of up to 5 lines is too long for a tooltip. Its panel (`.card-info`, a hairline above it, as View options' panel) holds the card's notes, their text and ids unchanged (a table's `aria-describedby` still names its subtitle): Authorized over time its subtitle and excluded note; Protection its note (the caveats); the breakdown its note and its excluded count; the conditions card its subtitle; Who is active where its subtitle and its current-owner note (the touch legend of the ATC columns stays visible); Approvals per year its note and its undated note. Open or closed is UI state. **Common Region**: every dashboard card has the same frame (`.chart-card`: 24px padding, 16px on phones, 12px corners, a hairline; the padding is `--card-pad`, which the Medicines `.table-card`'s head, caption and rows share since the design sweep's A1, 2026-10-01; every frame inside a lookup card, i.e. the medicine card's blocks, More details' summary and sections, `.card-section`, the result tables and the empty state, has `--card-pad-compact` at its sides: 16px, 14px on phones, 12px under 360px, the blocks' own values, so one card's text edges line up: 137px at 1440, 31px at 390, 29px at 320, where they stepped 137/141 and 31/33/37 before) and one head row (`.card-head`, at least 32px): the title with its (i) at the left (`.card-title`), its tools at the right (the breakdown's mode switch, View options, Download CSV), wrapping under it when short of room; then the takeaway, then the View options and (i) panels when open, then the content; 24px between cards, 16px from the tabs' hairline to the first card (8px margin plus the panel's 16px), and 32px from every tab's last card to the footer's hairline, which spans the page column as the tab strip's (design sweep A7 and A8, 2026-10-01: a last card's 24px margin made it 56px on every tab but the Overview, and the footer's border ran 32px past the column at each side at 1440, across the viewport below 1264px). The Overview's three previews keep their compact head (title, the tab link under it). **Von Restorff** (strong colour for status and protection only): ATC letter and code badges and segmented ATC badges are the neutral slate in the cards where they are decoration, as the code already names the class (`.neutral-badges`: the breakdown, Who is active where, the Overview's previews, the protection card, the medicines table and the headline's class path; since the design sweep of 2026-10-01 (B1) also the lookups' result tables (condition, substance, indication-text and company pages: the same medicine rows as the medicines table) and the company page's "ATC groups" (as the breakdown's and the Companies tab's letters); their level shades kept, as they tell the levels apart; slate text on slate 1-5 is a checked pair); kept in colour: status dots and pills, the protection card's bars (accent, and orphan pink), the type badges and tiles (the charts' type colours: their legend), the per-year chart's series and its legend's company badges (series colours), ATC stacks by group hue, the activity heat cells (a scale), and company monograms in their group's brand or hashed colour in every card (owner decision 2026-09-30, "brand colors"; phase 3 had made them slate in those cards), but slate in the medicine and substance cards' blocks (Laws of UX, second pass, owner decision 2026-09-30). The filter popovers' trees keep their hues (a picker, not a card).
- **Tabs** (phase 2; `url.js` `TABS`, `main.js` `renderTabs()`, copy `UI.tabs`): under the chip bar (which filters every tab, as the headline above it counts), a strip of six tabs in this order: Overview · Protection · Classes and areas · Companies · By year · Medicines (Serial Position / Pareto: Protection second, the conference question "is it still protected?"). **Pattern** (owner decision 2026-09-30, "Tabs as links (current) vs the ARIA tabs pattern → ARIA tabs"; phase 2 had links in a `<nav>`, the one shown `aria-current="page"`): the WAI-ARIA APG tabs pattern. `#tabs` is a `role="tablist"` named "Dashboard views" (`aria-label` from `UI.tabs.label`, set by `main.js`); each tab a `<button role="tab">` (`#tab-button-{tab}`, `aria-controls` its panel, `aria-selected` true on the tab shown); each panel a `role="tabpanel"` named by its tab (`aria-labelledby`) with `tabindex="0"`, as each starts with a card title, not a control (the APG asks for it then; its focus ring 4px outside its cards). One tab stop, roving: the tab shown has tabindex 0, the others -1 (`renderTabs()`); Left/Right move focus to the previous or next tab, wrapping, Home/End to the first and last (`tabsKeydown()`, `tabKeyTarget()` in `tabs.js`, pure part tested; modified keys left to the browser); Tab goes on to the panel. **Manual activation**: the keys only move focus; Enter, Space or a click (the button's own) shows the tab (`setState({ tab }, true)`: pushState, one history entry, focus stays on the tab), so a key press neither pushes a history entry nor renders a tab, as automatic activation would; back and forward restore the tab from the URL. The tabs have no `href` (no "open in a new tab" any more), but a direct link (`?tab=years`) still opens its tab and the previews' "… tab" links keep theirs; `document.title` names the tab ("By year · Approval Atlas"; the Overview none; a drug class shown alone first: "L04AC … · Classes and areas · …").
